        Implicit = 0,
        Explicit = 1,
        CheckedExplicit = 2,
        UnaryPlus = 3,
        UnaryNegation = 4,
        LogicalNot = 5,
        OnesComplement = 6,
        Increment = 7,
        Decrement = 8,
        True = 9,
        False = 10,
        Addition = 11,
        Subtraction = 12,
        Multiply = 13,
        Division = 14,
        Modulus = 15,
        BitwiseAnd = 16,
        BitwiseOr = 17,
        ExclusiveOr = 18,
        LeftShift = 19,
        RightShift = 20,
        UnsignedRightShift = 21,
        Equality = 22,
        Inequality = 23,
        LessThan = 24,
        GreaterThan = 25,
        LessThanOrEqual = 26,
